Second transcription's note: Derived from any person filing Schedule SE, the clergy flag, or the Schedule 1 statutory-employee gate s1.sc_1a.

Ruling: model error; i1040gi p. 42, Step 5, question 2: "Were you self-employed at any time in 2025, or are you filing Schedule SE because you were a member of the clergy or you had church employee income, or are you filing Schedule C as a statutory employee? Yes. Skip question 3 and Step 6; go to Worksheet B." Triage executable-triage-v2 (`../triage/eic.deepseek.json`; 1,374 fixtures, 500 generated returns seed 20261026, 131 partial records; 2,005 comparisons; every completed-return difference inspected with the 6-witness cap lifted, T-083): 1,754 of 2,005 agree; 169 value (55 on completed returns), 82 candidate-refuses (all on refused returns). The candidate counts only persons filing Schedule SE (person.files_se), plus a constant-false clergy flag and the statutory-employee gate; the project counts Schedules C (eic.sc_count). All 55 completed values are project yes / candidate no on returns with one or two Schedules C and no Schedule SE (classm.f4952.2, classm.f8880.2, classm.eic.3, classm.s8812.3, stage14.sc.loss, stage14.eic.wsb_loss, stage19.mut.se_not_filed_loss, generated 20, 150, 238 and others): a sole proprietor with a loss or a profit under the Schedule SE threshold was self-employed. The 82 candidate-refuses are Schedule C and SE gates on refused returns (scope). Project stands.

Ruling: model error; on the reply format. The prompt lists the line as printed '6 (eic.line6)' (id eic.line6), beside '6 (eic.wsb_6)'; the reply printed it '6', so the comparison found no line (MISSING) and triage could not run it. By reading, it would also be wrong on the merits: i1040gi p. 40, Step 1, question 1: "If, in 2025: 3 or more children who have valid SSNs lived with you, is the amount on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b, less than $61,555 ($68,675 if married filing jointly)? ... No. STOP You can’t take the credit." i1040gi p. 46, Worksheet A: "Use this worksheet only if you answered “No” to Step 5, question 2. Otherwise, use Worksheet B." The reply's eic.line6 is the Worksheet A choice of line 2 or the smaller of lines 2 and 5 with no Steps 1 to 5 and no shown_when, figured from a line 1 that ignores Worksheet B, so it shows a credit after every STOP (as its eic.credit, e.g. stage9.eic.no_child.over65 $308.00 where the project hides line 6). Project stands.

## Raw second transcription

```yaml
lines:
  - id: eic.step5_line1
    printed: "(Step 5, 1)"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 1z."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line1z}
  - id: eic.step5_line2
    printed: "(Step 5, 2)"
    label: "Enter the Medicaid waiver payment amounts excluded from income on Schedule 1, line 8s, unless you choose to include these amounts in earned income, in which case enter -0-."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [scope.other_income]
    note: "No Medicaid waiver input is supplied in the closed interface; zero is used, and scope.other_income refuses this line when other income including Medicaid waiver payments is present."
  - id: eic.step5_line3
    printed: "(Step 5, 3)"
    label: "Subtract line 2 from line 1."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: subtract, from: eic.step5_line1, take: eic.step5_line2}
  - id: eic.step5_line4
    printed: "(Step 5, 4)"
    label: "Enter all of your nontaxable combat pay if you elect to include it in earned income. Also enter the amount of your nontaxable combat pay on line 1i of Form 1040 or 1040-SR."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
    refuses: [s8812.combat_pay]
    note: "No combat-pay amount or election input is supplied in the closed interface; zero is used, and s8812.combat_pay refuses this line when combat pay is present."
  - id: eic.earned_income
    printed: "(Step 5, 5)"
    label: "Add lines 3 and 4. This is your earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.step5_line3, eic.step5_line4]}
  - id: eic.wsb_4b
    printed: "4b"
    label: "Combine lines 1e, 2c, 3, and 4a. This is your total earned income."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum, of: [eic.earned_income, eic.h_se_income_total]}
    note: "The archived Worksheet B combines lines 1e, 2c, 3 and 4a; those component lines are not in the closed interface, so the catalog self-employment earned-income aggregate is added to Step 5 earned income."
  - id: eic.wsb_6
    printed: "6 (eic.wsb_6)"
    label: "Enter your total earned income from Part 4, line 4b."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: subtract_floor0, from: eic.wsb_4b, take: {const: 0}}
    note: "Worksheet B stops if line 4b is zero or less; represented as a zero floor on line 6."
  - id: eic.line1
    printed: "1"
    label: "Enter your earned income from Step 5."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: eic.earned_income}
  - id: eic.line2
    printed: "2"
    label: "Look up the amount on line 1 above in the EIC Table to find the credit. Be sure you use the correct column for your filing status and the number of qualifying children you have who have valid SSNs as defined earlier. Enter the credit here."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_t0
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_t1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_t2
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line2_t3
    note: "The EIC Table column is chosen by the dependent.eic_child and dependent.eic_ssn_child count; three or more uses the eic_3 table."
  - id: eic.line3
    printed: "3"
    label: "Enter the amount from Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 11b."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: copy, from: f1040.line11b}
  - id: eic.line4
    printed: "4"
    label: "Are the amounts on lines 3 and 1 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [eic.line3, eic.line1]}
  - id: eic.line5
    printed: "5"
    label: "If the amount on line 3 is less than the applicable threshold, leave line 5 blank; otherwise look up the amount on line 3 in the EIC Table and enter the credit here. Then enter the smaller of line 5 and line 2 on line 6."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_below_threshold, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_zero
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_below_threshold, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_table
    note: "When the Part 2 threshold applies, the IRS says to leave line 5 blank; this model records the blank as zero and line 6 takes line 2."
  - id: eic.line6
    printed: "6"
    label: "This is your earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_below_threshold, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.line2
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.line4, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_line5_below_threshold, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_smaller
  - id: eic.clergy
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — clergy or church-employee flag from Step 5, question 1."
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [{const: false}, {const: true}]}
    note: "No clergy or church-employee input is supplied in the closed interface; this result is therefore set false and does not detect that case."
  - id: eic.self_employed
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Step 5, question 2: self-employed, clergy or church employee, or statutory employee."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_se_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.clergy, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [s1.sc_1a, {const: true}]}
    note: "Derived from any person filing Schedule SE, the clergy flag, or the Schedule 1 statutory-employee gate s1.sc_1a."
  - id: eic.separated_box
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — special rule for separated spouses box in the Dependents section."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      all:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: false}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfs"}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "hoh"}]}
        - op: test
          any:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.same_home, {const: false}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.legally_separated, {const: true}]}
    note: "Derived from Step 3's special rule for separated spouses; this does not separately test whether a qualifying child is present."
  - id: eic.credit
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — final 2025 earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.line6
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.self_employed, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line11
  - id: eic.check_27c
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — check the box on Form 1040 or 1040-SR, line 27c."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.qc_of_another, {const: true}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.us_home, {const: false}]}
        - {op: test, eq: [eic.credit, {const: 0}]}
    note: "Derived from the explicit line 27c STOP cases represented by the closed inputs and from a zero final credit."
  - id: eic.h_zero
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — zero amount."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: param, ref: ss.zero}
  - id: eic.h_qc_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — number of EIC qualifying children with valid SSNs."
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: dependent
      field: dependent.one
      where:
        op: test
        all:
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_child, {const: true}]}
          - {op: test, eq: [dependent.eic_ssn_child, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_se_income_total
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — total self-employment earned income from person entries."
    type: Dollars
    expr: {op: sum_over, collection: person, field: person.eic_se}
  - id: eic.h_se_count
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — number of persons filing Schedule SE."
    type: Count
    expr:
      op: sum_over
      collection: person
      field: person.one
      where: {op: test, eq: [person.files_se, {const: true}]}
  - id: eic.h_line2_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — EIC Table credit for line 1 with no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line2_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — EIC Table credit for line 1 with one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line2_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — EIC Table credit for line 1 with two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line2_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — EIC Table credit for line 1 with three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line1, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line5_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — EIC Table credit for line 3 with no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line5_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — EIC Table credit for line 3 with one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line5_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — EIC Table credit for line 3 with two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line5_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — EIC Table credit for line 3 with three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.line3, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_line5_table
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — EIC Table credit for line 3 by qualifying-child count."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_t0
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_t1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_t2
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_line5_t3
  - id: eic.h_line5_below_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — whether Worksheet A line 3 is below the Part 2 threshold."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.other}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}}
            - {op: test, lt: [eic.line3, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.other}]}
  - id: eic.h_line5_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — smaller of Worksheet A lines 5 and 2."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.line5, eic.line2]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line7_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 7 credit with no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line7_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 7 credit with one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line7_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 7 credit with two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line7_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 7 credit with three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: eic.wsb_6, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line7
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 7 credit by qualifying-child count."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line7_t0
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line7_t1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line7_t2
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line7_t3
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10_t0
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 10 credit with no qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_0, key: f1040.line11b, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10_t1
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 10 credit with one qualifying child."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_1, key: f1040.line11b, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10_t2
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 10 credit with two qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_2, key: f1040.line11b, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10_t3
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 10 credit with three or more qualifying children."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: table_lookup, table: eic_3, key: f1040.line11b, status: f1040.status}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 10 credit by qualifying-child count."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line10_t0
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line10_t1
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 2, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line10_t2
        - guard: {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 3, type: Count}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line10_t3
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line9
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 9: are lines 8 and 6 the same?"
    type: Bool
    expr: {op: test, eq: [f1040.line11b, eic.wsb_6]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_below_threshold
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — whether Worksheet B line 8 is below the Part 6 threshold."
    type: Bool
    expr:
      op: test
      any:
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 0, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.ws_threshold0.other}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.joint}]}
        - op: test
          all:
            - {op: test, ge: [eic.h_qc_count, {const: 1, type: Count}]}
            - {op: test, not: {op: test, eq: [f1040.status, {const: "mfj"}]}}
            - {op: test, lt: [f1040.line11b, {param: eic.ws_threshold1.other}]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line10_smaller
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — smaller of Worksheet B lines 10 and 7."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr: {op: smaller_of, of: [eic.h_wsb_line10, eic.h_wsb_line7]}
  - id: eic.h_wsb_line11
    printed: "(helper)"
    label: "Helper — Worksheet B line 11 earned income credit."
    type: NonNegDollars
    expr:
      op: choose
      alternatives:
        - guard: {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_line9, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line7
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_line9, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_below_threshold, {const: true}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line7
        - guard:
            op: test
            all:
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_line9, {const: false}]}
              - {op: test, eq: [eic.h_wsb_below_threshold, {const: false}]}
          then: eic.h_wsb_line10_smaller
```
